Feasibility projection: changing one coordinate can violate sum. Coordinate descent should maintain sum exactly: If we move coordinate i to z, to keep sum constant, we must adjust other coordinates. Our coordinate update as defined changes sum by δ, violating constraint. We need account! The problem has equality sum = N/2. Coordinate descent must update while preserving sum. If we change one coordinate and don't adjust sum, invalid. We need either:
- Allow one coordinate and after each update project sum (changes all coords), invalidating c update (would need recompute c or update all affected). That breaks coordinate exact.
- Pair update: change coordinate i and distribute compensation over others. `smooth_max_value(corr, beta)`, `smooth_max_weights(corr, beta)` as previous. For beta large, numerical issues: use m=maxcorr; shifted=beta*(corr-m); clip shifted? Since corr-m≤0. shifted <=0. `project_feasible(h, target=None)`:
```
def project_feasible(h, target=None):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    n = h.size
    if target is None: target = 0.5*n
    if n == 0: return h
    # clip first maybe helps
    h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # bisection alpha
    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    # expand to ensure bracket
    while np.clip(h+lo,0,1).sum() > target:  # lo too high? Actually lower alpha sum smaller.
        lo *=2; if lo<-4: break
    while np.clip(h+hi,0,1).sum() < target:
        hi *=2; if hi>4: break
    # Set lo to target - sum(n?); Better: lo=-1,hi=1 usually enough because h in[0,1] target in[0,n]; shifting by ±1 works: if alpha=-1 all zeros, sum0; if +1 all ones, sum n. Actually h+alpha clipped: alpha=-1 may not all zeros if h=1 ->0; yes min 0. alpha=1 all ones. So bracket [-1,1] works.
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid=(lo+hi)/2
        s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
        if s > target: hi=mid
        else: lo=mid
    h=np.clip(h+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       # adjust coordinate with most slack
       slack=np.minimum(h,1.0-h)
       idx=np.argmax(slack)
       if slack[idx] > abs(diff)*1.01:
           h[idx]+=diff
       else:
           # distribute among all with slack
           mask=slack>1e-12
           if np.any(mask): h[mask]+=diff/np.sum(mask)
           else: h[0]+=diff
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       # final tiny adjust again maybe
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)>0:
           idx=np.argmax(np.minimum(h,1-h))
           h[idx]=np.clip(h[idx]+diff,0,1)
    return h
```
But bisection condition: sum(alpha) increasing in alpha. We want s=target. If s>target, need lower alpha -> hi=mid; else lo=mid. Starting lo=-1 sum0≤target, hi=1 sum n≥target. target could be 0 or n; but target n/2, okay. 60 iterations enough double precision? n up to 512, sum error ~n*eps*? 1e-12. Good.

Smooth objective/gradient:
```
def smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr,beta):
    m=np.max(corr)
    z=corr-m
    scaled=beta*z
    scaled=np.clip(scaled,-50.0,0.0)
    exps=np.exp(scaled)
    total=exps.sum()
    vals=m + np.log(total)/beta
    w=exps/total
    return vals,w
```
If beta huge and all z negative except max, total ~1, w on max. Good.

`smooth_full_obj(x,beta)`:

```
`smooth_full_jac(x,beta)`:

n=x.size; corr=...; val,w=...; grad=zeros; dx=2/n
for idx in range(len(w)):
    wt=w[idx]
    if wt<1e-14: continue
    lag=idx-(n-1)
    if lag==0: grad += wt*(1-2*x)
    elif lag>0: d=lag; grad[d:] += wt*(1-x[:n-d]); grad[:n-d] -= wt*x[d:]
    else: d=-lag; grad[:n-d] += wt*(1-x[d:]); grad[d:] -= wt*x[:n-d]
return dx*grad
```
This loops over 2n-1 every jac; okay. We can skip if beta low weights all small? Weights sum1; many small. Use threshold 1e-12. Good.

def full_sum_fun(x): return np.sum(x)-x.size/2.0
def full_sum_jac(x): return np.ones_like(x)
def sym_sum_fun(y,n): return np.sum(y)-n/4.0

```

   bounds=[(0,1)]*m

def interp_h(h_src,n_dst):
    n_src=len(h_src); if n_src==n_dst: return project_feasible(h_src.copy(),0.5*n_dst)
    xs=(arange(n_src)+0.5)*(2/n_src); xd=(arange(n_dst)+0.5)*(2/n_dst)

`local_binary_search(h_int,n,max_time)`:
Inside uses global time. Implement carefully.

```
def local_binary_search(h_int, n, max_time):
    h = h_int.astype(np.int8).copy()
    # ensure M ones; if not threshold
    M=n//2
    cnt=int(np.sum(h))
    if cnt != M:
       h = binary_vector_from_float(h.astype(float))
    c = counts_int(h); best=int(np.max(c)); evals=0; no_improve=0
    while elapsed remaining > 5 and no_improve < 20000:
       # recompute top every 200 evals
       if evals%200==0 or top_lags is None:
          top_lags = np.argsort(c)[-8:]
          ones=np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]
       improved=False
       # attempt e.g. 200 trials
       for _ in range(200):
          if remaining<=5: break
          # choose move
          if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
          # 70% active bad pair
          if rng.random()<0.7:
             li=top_lags[rng.randint(len(top_lags))]
             offset=li-(n-1)
             if offset>=0:
                if offset==0: continue
                n0=np.arange(0,n-offset)
                a=n0+offset; b=n0
             else:
                d=-offset; n0=np.arange(d,n)
                a=n0+offset; b=n0
             bad=(h[a]==1)&(h[b]==0)
             if not np.any(bad):
                # random one zero
             else:
                pos=np.where(bad)[0]
                sel=pos[rng.randint(len(pos))]
                p=int(a[sel]); q=int(b[sel])
          else:
              p=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; q=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
          if p==q: continue
          # swap
          old_p=h[p]; old_q=h[q]
          h[p]=h[q]; h[q]=old_p
          c_new = counts_int(h); mc=int(np.max(c_new)); evals+=1
          if mc < best:
             best=mc; c=c_new; h stays; no_improve=0; improved=True
              # maybe if best == lower bound break? if best == ceil(n/8) can't improve? We can break
              if best <= max(0,int(np.ceil(n/8.0))-1? Actually lower bound ceil(N^2/(4(2N-2))). If best == lower_bound, optimal? Could break. But compute lower bound.
          else:
             h[p]=old_p; h[q]=old_q
          if not improved and evals - last_improve > 5000: kick...
```
Need update ones/zeros after accept? For simplicity recompute top every 200 evals; ones/zeros also. If accept within batch, ones/zeros may stale; but okay. At recompute, update.
Kick: if no_improve > 5000, do random 5 swaps (even if worse), recompute c,best? or reset no_improve. This can escape. Also if c max high, try re-initialization? We can outside restart local search with new start if no improve too long.

`counts_int(h)` returns int16 array. For `np.correlate` on int8, 1-h may be negative if h int8? 1-h int8 okay values 0/1. Use int16 for output. `h16=h.astype(np.int16); return np.correlate(h16, (1-h16).astype(np.int16), mode='full')`. Counts int16. For n up to 512 fits.

Use np.correlate(a,v) c_idx =∑ a[n+lag]v[n], lag=idx-(N-1). For lag>0: terms i=n+lag from lag to N-1, j=n from 0 to N-1-lag. c=∑_{j=0}^{N-1-lag} h[j+lag](1-h[j]). Deriv wrt h_i:
If i=j+lag, i∈[lag,N-1], coeff 1-h[i-lag]. If i=j, i∈[0,N-1-lag], coeff -h[i+lag]. Our update for lag>0: grad[l:] += w*(1-h[:N-l]) -> for i=l..N-1, h_i-l. grad[:N-l] -= w*h[l:] -> for i=0..N-1-l, h_{i+l}. Good.
For lag<0 with d=-lag: c=∑_{j=d}^{N-1} h[j-d](1-h[j]) =∑_{i=0}^{N-d-1} h[i](1-h[i+d]). Deriv: i∈[0,N-d-1] coeff 1-h[i+d]; i∈[d,N-1] coeff -h[i-d]. Our update for lag<0: d=-lag; grad[:N-d]+=w*(1-h[d:]) -> i=0..N-d-1, h_{i+d}; grad[d:]-=w*h[:N-d] -> i=d..N-1, h_{i-d}. Good.
Lag=0: c0=∑ h_i(1-h_i), deriv 1-2h_i. Good.

Let's design final code with functions and `run` orchestrating.

Potential issue with `minimize` args for constraints: When using `minimize` with SLSQP, if `jac` for constraint returns a scalar? It should return array. We'll return `np.ones(n)`, not `np.ones(n).reshape(1,-1)`. Good.

Let's write code carefully.

Detailed function implementations:

`smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, beta)`:
```
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    z = corr - m
    scaled = beta * z
    np.clip(scaled, -50.0, 0.0, out=scaled)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    total = float(exps.sum())
    val = m + np.log(total) / beta
    w = exps / total
    return val, w
```
If beta <=0? not.

`grad_from_weights(h, weights)`:
```
    n=len(h); grad=np.zeros(n)
    off=n-1
    for idx, wt in enumerate(weights):
       if wt <= 1e-14: continue
       lag = idx - off
       if lag == 0:
          grad += wt * (1.0 - 2.0*h)
       elif lag >0:
          d=lag
          grad[d:] += wt * (1.0 - h[:n-d])
          grad[:n-d] -= wt * h[d:]
       else:
          d=-lag
          grad[:n-d] += wt * (1.0 - h[d:])
          grad[d:] -= wt * h[:n-d]
    return grad
```
This loops zeros; okay. We can maybe only iterate nonzero weights >1e-15.

`full_obj(x,beta)` returns smooth value*dx. `full_jac(x,beta)`:
```
    n=x.size; corr=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full'); val,w=...; grad=grad_from_weights(x,w); return (2.0/n)*grad
```

`make_h_sym(y,n)`:
```
    m=n//2
    h=np.empty(n,float)
    h[:m]=y
    h[m:]=y[::-1]
    return h
```
Assumes y length m.
`sym_obj(y,beta,n)`, `sym_jac(y,beta,n)` as above.
For n even. If n odd, m=(n-1)//2, center? Avoid odd.

Constraints:
```
def full_sum_fun(x): return np.sum(x) - 0.5*x.size
def full_sum_jac(x): return np.ones(x.size, dtype=float)
def sym_sum_fun(y,n): return np.sum(y) - 0.25*n
def sym_sum_jac(y,n): return np.ones(y.size, dtype=float)
```
Note `0.25*n` is n/4. For n even, feasible target may be fractional? n even => n/4 maybe .5 if n mod4=2. y sum can be fractional, okay.

`run_slsqp_single(h0,n,beta,maxiter,sym)`:
- Check `maxiter` int >0.
- For sym: if n<4 or n%2!=0: use full. m=n//2; y=h0[:m]; target=n/4; y=project_feasible(y,target). If target not within [0,m], project.
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sym_sum_fun,'jac':sym_sum_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   try res=minimize(smooth_sym_obj, y, args=(beta,n), jac=smooth_sym_jac, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-13})

`make_lowfreq_continuous(n,rng,modes=4)`:
```
t = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
h = np.full(n,0.5)
for _ in range(modes):
   f = rng.uniform(0.5,4.0)
   amp = rng.uniform(0.15,0.55)
   ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
   s = 1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0
   h += s*amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t+ph)
return project_feasible(h,0.5*n)

def enumerate_small_binary(n, max_time):
    m=n//2
    best= n # large
    lb = max(1, int(np.ceil(n*n/(4.0*(2*n-2)))) if n>1 else 0)
    limit_mask=(1<<n)-1
    count=0
    start=time.time()
    # use combinations
    from itertools import combinations # import at top maybe
    for comb in combinations(range(n), m):
        if time.time()-start > max_time: break
        if remaining_s() <20: break
        mask=0
        for i in comb: mask |= 1<<i
        z = limit_mask ^ mask
        # early if best==lb break all
        ok=True
        for d in range(1,n):
            pd=(mask & (z >> d)).bit_count()
            if pd >= best: ok=False; break
            qd=(mask & ((z << d) & limit_mask)).bit_count()
            if qd >= best: ok=False; break
        if ok:
            # need actual max if best not updated? If no break, max < best. Re-evaluate max to update best? We can compute maxc by loop again or store max during. Store maxc.
            # Better in loop track maxc and break if >=best. If finish, maxc is max.
            best=maxc
            if best == lb: break
    return best
```

`project_feasible`: Need avoid lo/hi arithmetic with target exact. Let's implement robust:
```
def project_feasible(h, target=None):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    n=h.size
    if n==0: return h
    if target is None: target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # If sum close, adjust maybe
    def not allowed nested, so just code inline.
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    # ensure bracket maybe if target near 0 n
    s_lo=np.clip(h+lo,0,1).sum(); s_hi=np.clip(h+hi,0,1).sum()
    # s_lo should <= target <= s_hi. If not due target? expand:
    while s_lo > target and lo > -10.0:
        lo *= 2.0; s_lo=np.clip(h+lo,0,1).sum()
    while s_hi < target and hi < 10.0:
        hi *= 2.0; s_hi=np.clip(h+hi,0,1).sum()
    for _ in range(60):
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
        if s > target: hi=mid
        else: lo=mid
    h=np.clip(h+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
        slack=np.minimum(h,1.0-h)
        idx=int(np.argmax(slack))
        if slack[idx] > abs(diff):
            h[idx]+=diff
        else:
            mask=slack>1e-12
            cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt>0:
                h[mask]+=diff/cnt
            else:
                h[0]+=diff
        h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>0:
        slack=np.minimum(h,1.0-h)
        idx=int(np.argmax(slack))
        h[idx]=float(np.clip(h[idx]+diff,0,1))
    return h
```
Nested? No, just code.
But `s_lo` with lo=-1: if h all 1 and target n, s_lo=0 <= target; hi=1 s_hi=n. Good.

def enumerate_binary_counts(n, max_time=20.0):
    m=n//2
    if m==0: return 0
    limit_mask=(1<<n)-1
    best=n
    lb = int(np.ceil((n*n)/(4.0*(2.0*n-2.0)))) if n>1 else 0
    start=time.time()
    for comb in combinations(range(n), m):
        if time.time() - start > max_time: break
        if remaining_s() < 20: break
        mask=0
        for i in comb: mask |= 1 << i
        z=limit_mask ^ mask
        maxc=0
        for d in range(1,n):
            p=(mask & (z >> d)).bit_count()
            if p > maxc: maxc=p
            if maxc >= best: break
            q=(mask & ((z << d) & limit_mask)).bit_count()
            if q > maxc: maxc=q
            if maxc >= best: break
        if maxc < best:
            best=maxc
            if best == lb: break
    return best
```

```

def local_binary_search(h_in,n,max_time=60.0):
    h=np.asarray(h_in,dtype=np.int8).copy()
    m=n//2
    cnt=int(np.sum(h))
    if cnt != m:
       h = binary_from_float(h.astype(float)).astype(np.int8)
    c = counts_int(h); cur=int(np.max(c)); best=cur; best_h=h.copy(); start=time.time(); no=0; top_infos=None; ones=zeros=None
    while time.time()-start < max_time and remaining_s()>20:
        if top_infos is None or no>100? update
        if top_infos is None:
           top = np.argsort(c)[-6:]
           infos=[]
           for li in top:
           offset=li-(n-1)
           if offset==0: continue
           if offset>0:
              n0=np.arange(n-offset); a=n0+offset; b=n0
           else:
              d=-offset; n0=np.arange(d,n); a=n0+offset; b=n0
           infos.append((a,b))
           top_infos=infos
           ones=np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]
        for _ in range(200):
           if ...
           if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
           p=q=None
           if len(top_infos)>0 and rng.random()<0.75:
               a,b=top_infos[rng.randint(len(top_infos))]
               bad=(h[a]==1)&(h[b]==0)
               if np.any(bad):
                   pos=np.where(bad)[0]
                   sel=pos[rng.randint(len(pos))]
                   p=int(a[sel]); q=int(b[sel])
           if p is None:
               p=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]); q=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
           if p==q: continue
           oldp=h[p]; oldq=h[q]
           h[p]=h[q]; h[q]=oldp
           c_new=counts_int(h); new=int(np.max(c_new)); no+=1
           if new < cur or (new == cur and rng.random()<0.05):
               cur=new; c=c_new
               if new < best:
                   best=new; best_h=h.copy()
                   no=0
                   # if best <= lower bound? break
                   lb=...; if best==lb: return best_h.astype(float), best*2.0/n
               # tops stale; maybe recompute after 50 accept? use no
               if no%100==0? no reset, need counter evals?
           else:
               h[p]=oldp; h[q]=oldq
               no+=1? Already no? We increment per trial. Let's use evals separate.
        if no > 4000:
           # kick from best
           kick_h=best_h.copy()
           ones_k=np.where(kick_h==1)[0]; zeros_k=np.where(kick_h==0)[0]
           for _ in range(rng.randint(3,8)):
              if len(ones_k)==0 or len(zeros_k)==0: break
              p=int(ones_k[rng.randint(len(ones_k))]); q=int(zeros_k[rng.randint(len(zeros_k))])
              kick_h[p]=0; kick_h[q]=1
           h=kick_h; c=counts_int(h); cur=int(np.max(c)); top_infos=None; no=0
    return best_h.astype(float), best*2.0/n
```

def pair_local_search(h_in,n,max_time=60.0):

       for _ in range(100):

              a,b=random info
              # sample 4 pairs and choose max h[a]*(1-h[b])
              sample = rng.randint(len(a),4)
              best_s=-1; for idx in sample: s=h[a[idx]]*(1-h[b[idx]]); if s>best_s: best_s=s; p=a[idx]; q=b[idx]

              else random p,q
              if h[p] <= h[q]: fallback random?

          if max_alpha <=1e-8: continue

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
    _STATE['start']=time.time(); _STATE['budget']=float(budget_s)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    parent=None; parent_n=0

       pass

         n0=200; h0=make_lowfreq_continuous(n0,rng,5); update_best(h0,n0)

    final=compute_c5(best_h)
    try: ce=evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h); if ce<final: final=ce
    except: pass
    return (best_h, float(final), int(_STATE['best_n']))

`smooth_sym_jac`: y length m. Need if `n` less than 2? not.
```
def smooth_sym_jac(y, beta, n):
    h=make_h_sym(y,n)
    corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
    val,w=smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr,beta)
    grad_full=grad_from_weights(h,w)
    m=n//2
    grad = grad_full[:m] + grad_full[m:][::-1]
    return (2.0/n)*grad
```
If n even. Good.

`run_slsqp_single`:
```
def run_slsqp_single(h0,n,beta,maxiter,sym):
    h0=np.asarray(h0,dtype=float); n=int(n)
    if n%2!=0: sym=False
    if sym and n>=4:
       m=n//2; target=n/4.0
       y=h0[:m].copy()
       if y.size != m: y=np.resize(y,m)
       y=project_feasible(y,target)
       bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
       cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sym_sum_fun,'jac':sym_sum_jac}]
       _SYM_N=n
       try:
          res=minimize(smooth_sym_obj, y, args=(float(beta), int(n)), jac=smooth_sym_jac, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-12})
       except Exception:
          pass
       yf=getattr(res,'x',y)
       y=project_feasible(np.asarray(yf,dtype=float),target)
       h=make_h_sym(y,n)
       h=project_feasible(h,0.5*n)
       h=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
       h=project_feasible(h,0.5*n)
    else:
       h=project_feasible(h0,0.5*n)
       bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
       cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':full_sum_fun,'jac':full_sum_jac}]
       try:
          res=minimize(full_obj,h,args=(float(beta),),jac=full_jac,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-12})
       except Exception:
          pass
       h=project_feasible(getattr(res,'x',h),0.5*n)
    return h, compute_c5(h)
```
If SLSQP res has no x? It always has. If exception before res, y unchanged. Good.
But if exception in sym, `res` undefined; `getattr(res,'x',y)` would NameError. Initialize `res=None` before try. Use `yf = getattr(res,'x',None) if res is not None else y`. Good.

`interp_h`: For n_dst maybe < n_src, x arrays. If source length 0? handle.
```
def interp_h(h_src,n_dst):
   n_src=len(h_src)
   if n_src==0: return np.zeros(n_dst)
   if n_src==n_dst: return project_feasible(h_src.copy(),0.5*n_dst)
   xs=(np.arange(n_src)+0.5)*(2.0/n_src)
   xd=(np.arange(n_dst)+0.5)*(2.0/n_dst)
   h=np.interp(xd,xs,h_src)
   return project_feasible(h,0.5*n_dst)
```

`binary_from_float`: if n odd? Use m=(n+1)//2? But sum constraint for odd not exact integer? We only use n even for binary. But safe: m=int(round(n/2))? For binary sum must n/2; if n odd impossible binary. Return maybe m=n//2. We'll only call even.
```
def binary_from_float(h, n=None):
   h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
   n=len(h) if n is None else int(n)
   m=n//2
   out=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
   if m<=0: return out
   if m>=n: out.fill(1); return out
   idx=np.argpartition(h, -m)[-m:]
   out[idx]=1.0
   return out
```
If n odd and m=n//2, sum=m not n/2; but not used. For safety, if n odd use continuous? Don't use.

`binary_from_mask`:
```
def binary_from_mask(mask,n):
   h=np.zeros(n)
   for i in range(n):
      if (mask >> i) & 1: h[i]=1.0
   return h
```

`counts_int(h)`:
```
def counts_int(h):
   h16=h.astype(np.int16, copy=False)
   return np.correlate(h16, (1 - h16).astype(np.int16, copy=False), mode='full')
```
If h int8 values 0/1, 1-h16 int16 okay. If h not exactly 0/1? Binary local ensures. For n>32767? no.

Can we compute smooth weights and gradient more efficiently? For all lags with weights, gradient contributions can be accumulated by convolution? For nonzero lags, each absolute d has two directed lags with weights w_pos and w_neg. The gradient for lag orientation is:
- for np lag +d: grad[d:] += w(1-h[:n-d]); grad[:n-d] -= w*h[d:]
- for np lag -d: grad[:n-d] += w(1-h[d:]); grad[d:] -= w*h[:n-d]
For d=0: grad += w0(1-2h).
For each absolute d, let wpos = weight at idx offset d, wneg = weight at idx offset -d. Combine:
For indices i:
    grad[i] from +d: if i>=d: +wpos*(1-h[i-d]); if i<n-d: -wpos*h[i+d]
    from -d: if i<n-d: +wneg*(1-h[i+d]); if i>=d: -wneg*h[i-d]
Can combine slices:
- seg A indices 0..d-1: only -d as second? and +d as second? Let's derive combined arrays:
For all i:
A = zeros
For +d: plus array P: P[d:]=1-h[:n-d]; P[:n-d]-=h[d:]
For -d: plus array Q: Q[:n-d]=1-h[d:]; Q[d:]-=h[:n-d]
Then grad += wpos*P + wneg*Q.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize
from itertools import combinations

_STATE = {
    "start": 0.0,
    "budget": 1300.0,
    "best_h": None,
    "best_c": float("inf"),
    "best_n": 0,
    "parent_h": None,
}
_SYM_N = 200
_TARGET = 0.38085


def elapsed_s():
    return time.time() - _STATE["start"]


def remaining_s():
    return max(0.0, _STATE["budget"] - elapsed_s())


def update_best(h, n, c=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return
    h = project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n)
    if c is None:
        c = compute_c5(h)
    else:
        c = min(float(c), compute_c5(h))
    if c + 1e-15 < _STATE["best_c"]:
        _STATE["best_h"] = h.copy()
        _STATE["best_c"] = float(c)
        _STATE["best_n"] = n
        print("improved: n={}, c5={:.6f}".format(n, c))


def project_feasible(h, target=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return h
    if target is None:
        target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    s_lo = np.clip(h + lo, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
    s_hi = np.clip(h + hi, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
    while s_lo > target and lo > -10.0:
        lo *= 2.0
        s_lo = np.clip(h + lo, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
    while s_hi < target and hi < 10.0:
        hi *= 2.0
        s_hi = np.clip(h + hi, 0.0, 1.0).sum()

    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid

    h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        slack = np.minimum(h, 1.0 - h)
        idx = int(np.argmax(slack))
        if slack[idx] > abs(diff) * 1.001:
            h[idx] += diff
        else:
            mask = slack > 1e-12
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt > 0:
                h[mask] += diff / cnt
            else:
                h[0] += diff
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        slack = np.minimum(h, 1.0 - h)
        idx = int(np.argmax(slack))
        h[idx] = float(np.clip(h[idx] + diff, 0.0, 1.0))
    return h


def symmetrize(h, n=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size) if n is None else int(n)
    s = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(s, 0.5 * n)


def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / n))


def correlate_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def counts_int(h):
    h16 = h.astype(np.int16, copy=False)
    v16 = (1 - h16).astype(np.int16, copy=False)
    return np.correlate(h16, v16, mode="full")


def interp_h(h_src, n_dst):
    h_src = np.asarray(h_src, dtype=float)
    n_src = h_src.size
    n_dst = int(n_dst)
    if n_src == 0:
        return np.zeros(n_dst, dtype=float)
    if n_src == n_dst:
        return project_feasible(h_src.copy(), 0.5 * n_dst)
    xs = (np.arange(n_src) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_src)
    xd = (np.arange(n_dst) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_dst)
    h = np.interp(xd, xs, h_src)
    return project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n_dst)


def binary_from_float(h, n=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size) if n is None else int(n)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return out
    if m >= n:
        out.fill(1.0)
        return out
    idx = np.argpartition(h, -m)[-m:]
    out[idx] = 1.0
    return out


def binary_from_mask(mask, n):
    h = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=float)
    for i in range(int(n)):
        if (mask >> i) & 1:
            h[i] = 1.0
    return h


def make_lowfreq_continuous(n, rng, modes=4):
    t = (np.arange(int(n)) + 0.5) / float(n)
    h = np.full(int(n), 0.5, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(int(modes)):
        f = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 4.0))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.12, 0.55))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        sgn = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
        h += sgn * amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + ph)
    return project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n)


def make_lowfreq_binary(n, rng, modes=5):
    h = make_lowfreq_continuous(int(n), rng, modes)
    return binary_from_float(h, int(n))


def smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, beta):
    corr = np.asarray(corr, dtype=float)
    beta = float(beta)
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    scaled = beta * (corr - m)
    np.clip(scaled, -50.0, 0.0, out=scaled)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    total = float(exps.sum())
    val = m + np.log(total) / beta
    w = exps / total
    return val, w


def grad_from_weights(h, weights):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    off = n - 1
    w0 = weights[off]
    if abs(w0) > 1e-15:
        g += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    one_h = 1.0 - h
    for d in range(1, n):
        wp = weights[off + d]
        wn = weights[off - d]
        if wp <= 1e-14 and wn <= 1e-14:
            continue
        a = n - d
        if wn != 0.0:
            g[:a] += wn * one_h[d:]
        if wp != 0.0:
            g[:a] -= wp * h[d:]
        if wp != 0.0:
            g[d:] += wp * one_h[:a]
        if wn != 0.0:
            g[d:] -= wn * h[:a]
    return g


def full_obj(x, beta):
    n = x.size
    corr = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    val, _ = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, float(beta))
    return val * (2.0 / n)


def full_jac(x, beta):
    n = x.size
    corr = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    _, w = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, float(beta))
    g = grad_from_weights(x, w)
    return g * (2.0 / n)


def full_sum_fun(x):
    return np.array([np.sum(x) - 0.5 * x.size])


def full_sum_jac(x):
    return np.ones((1, x.size))


def make_h_sym(y, n):
    m = n // 2
    h = np.empty(int(n), dtype=float)
    h[:m] = y
    h[m:] = y[::-1]
    return h


def smooth_sym_obj(y, beta, n):
    h = make_h_sym(y, int(n))
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    val, _ = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, float(beta))
    return val * (2.0 / n)


def smooth_sym_jac(y, beta, n):
    n = int(n)
    h = make_h_sym(y, n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    _, w = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, float(beta))
    gf = grad_from_weights(h, w)
    m = n // 2
    g = gf[:m] + gf[m:][::-1]
    return g * (2.0 / n)


def sym_sum_fun(y):
    return np.array([np.sum(y) - 0.25 * _SYM_N])


def sym_sum_jac(y):
    return np.ones((1, y.size))


def run_slsqp_single(h0, n, beta, maxiter, sym):
    h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    beta = float(beta)
    it = max(1, int(maxiter))
    sym = bool(sym) and (n % 2 == 0) and n >= 4
    res = None

    if sym:
        m = n // 2
        target = 0.25 * n
        y = h0[:m].copy()
        if y.size != m:
            y = np.resize(y, m)
        y = project_feasible(y, target)
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m
        cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sym_sum_fun, "jac": sym_sum_jac}]
        _SYM_N = n
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_sym_obj,
                y,
                args=(beta, n),
                jac=smooth_sym_jac,
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": it, "ftol": 1e-12},
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

        yf = getattr(res, "x", None) if res is not None else None
        if yf is None:
            yf = y
        y = project_feasible(np.asarray(yf, dtype=float), target)
        h = make_h_sym(y, n)
        h = project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n)
        h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
        h = project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n)
    else:
        h = project_feasible(h0, 0.5 * n)
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
        cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": full_sum_fun, "jac": full_sum_jac}]
        try:
            res = minimize(
                full_obj,
                h,
                args=(beta,),
                jac=full_jac,
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": it, "ftol": 1e-12},
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

        xf = getattr(res, "x", None) if res is not None else None
        if xf is None:
            xf = h
        h = project_feasible(np.asarray(xf, dtype=float), 0.5 * n)

    return h, compute_c5(h)


def run_smooth_sequence(h0, n, betas, iters, sym, deadline=0.0):
    h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return h0, float("inf")
    h = project_feasible(h0, 0.5 * n)
    c = compute_c5(h)
    update_best(h, n, c)

    for i in range(len(betas)):
        if deadline > 0.0 and time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if remaining_s() < 45.0:
            break
        try:
            nh, nc = run_slsqp_single(h, n, betas[i], iters[i], sym)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if nc < c - 1e-13:
            h = nh
            c = nc
            update_best(h, n, c)
    return h, c


def enumerate_binary_counts(n, max_time=20.0):
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return 0, 0
    limit_mask = (1 << n) - 1
    best = n + 1
    best_mask = 0
    if n > 1:
        lb = int(np.ceil((n * n) / (4.0 * (2.0 * n - 2.0))))
    else:
        lb = 0
    start = time.time()

    for comb in combinations(range(n), m):
        if start and (time.time() - start) > max_time:
            break
        if remaining_s() < 20.0:
            break
        mask = 0
        for ii in comb:
            mask |= 1 << ii
        z = limit_mask ^ mask
        maxc = 0
        for d in range(1, n):
            pc = (mask & (z >> d)).bit_count()
            if pc > maxc:
                maxc = pc
            if maxc >= best:
                break
            qc = (mask & ((z << d) & limit_mask)).bit_count()
            if qc > maxc:
                maxc = qc
            if maxc >= best:
                break
        if maxc < best:
            best = maxc
            best_mask = mask
            if best == lb:
                break
    return best, best_mask


def small_n_track(rng):
    start = time.time()
    total = min(200.0, 0.18 * _STATE["budget"])
    deadline = time.time() + total
    ns = [6, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20]

    for n in ns:
        if time.time() > deadline or remaining_s() < 80.0:
            break
        cnt = None
        mask = None
        if n <= 20:
            et = 10.0 if n <= 14 else 25.0
            et = max(4.0, min(et, deadline - time.time() - 60.0))
            if et > 3.0 and remaining_s() > et + 40.0:
                try:
                    cnt, mask = enumerate_binary_counts(n, et)
                except Exception:
                    cnt, mask = None, None
                if mask is not None:
                    h = binary_from_mask(mask, n)
                    update_best(h, n, cnt * 2.0 / n)

        if time.time() > deadline - 80.0 or remaining_s() < 70.0:
            continue

        starts = []
        if mask is not None:
            starts.append(binary_from_mask(mask, n))
        starts.append(make_lowfreq_continuous(n, rng, 5))
        if _STATE["best_h"] is not None and _STATE["best_h"].size > 1:
            starts.append(interp_h(_STATE["best_h"], n))

        betas = [20.0, 60.0, 150.0]
        iters = [120, 150, 180]
        for h0 in starts[:3]:
            if time.time() > deadline - 30.0 or remaining_s() < 50.0:
                break
            try:
                run_smooth_sequence(h0, n, betas, iters, False, deadline=deadline)
            except Exception:
                pass


def local_binary_search(h_in, n, max_time=60.0):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.asarray(h_in, dtype=np.int8).copy()
    m = n // 2
    if int(np.sum(h)) != m:
        h = binary_from_float(h.astype(float), n).astype(np.int8)

    c = counts_int(h)
    cur = int(np.max(c))
    best = cur
    best_h = h.copy()
    start = time.time()
    evals = 0
    last_best = 0
    top_infos = None
    lb = int(np.ceil((n * n) / (4.0 * (2.0 * n - 2.0)))) if n > 1 else 0

    while time.time() - start < max_time and remaining_s() > 20.0:
        if top_infos is None or evals % 300 == 0:
            top_idx = np.argsort(c)[-6:]
            infos = []
            off = n - 1
            for li in top_idx:
                li = int(li)
                lag = li - off
                if lag == 0:
                    continue
                if lag > 0:
                    n0 = np.arange(n - lag)
                    a = n0 + lag
                    b = n0
                else:
                    d = -lag
                    n0 = np.arange(d, n)
                    a = n0 + lag
                    b = n0
                infos.append((a, b))
            top_infos = infos

        for _ in range(250):
            if time.time() - start > max_time or remaining_s() < 20.0:
                break
            p = None
            q = None

            if top_infos is not None and len(top_infos) > 0 and rng_local is not None:
                pass

        break

    # The placeholder above cannot be used; implement actual loop below.
    return best_h.astype(float), best * (2.0 / n)

def local_binary_search(h_in, n, rng, max_time=60.0):
    n=int(n)
    h=np.asarray(h_in,dtype=np.int8).copy()
    m=n//2
    if int(np.sum(h)) != m:
       h = binary_from_float(h.astype(float),n).astype(np.int8)
    c=counts_int(h)
    cur=int(np.max(c))
    best=cur
    best_h=h.copy()
    start=time.time()
    evals=0
    last_best=0
    top_infos=None
    lb=int(np.ceil((n*n)/(4.0*(2.0*n-2.0)))) if n>1 else 0

    while time.time()-start < max_time and remaining_s() > 20.0:
       if top_infos is None or evals % 300 == 0:
          off=n-1
          top_idx=np.argsort(c)[-6:]
          infos=[]
          for li in top_idx:
             li=int(li)
             lag=li-off
             if lag==0: continue
             if lag>0:
                 n0=np.arange(n-lag)
                 a=n0+lag
                 b=n0
             else:
                 d=-lag
                 n0=np.arange(d,n)
                 a=n0+lag
                 b=n0
             infos.append((a,b))
          top_infos=infos

       for _ in range(250):
          if time.time()-start > max_time or remaining_s() < 20.0: break
          p = -1; q = -1
          if top_infos is not None and len(top_infos) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.75:
             a,b = top_infos[int(rng.randint(len(top_infos)))]
             bad = (h[a] == 1) & (h[b] == 0)
             if np.any(bad):
                 pos = np.where(bad)[0]
                 sel = pos[int(rng.randint(len(pos)))]
                 p = int(a[sel]); q = int(b[sel])
          if p < 0:
             ok=False
             for __ in range(10):
                 rr=int(rng.randint(n)); ss=int(rng.randint(n))
                 if rr != ss and h[rr] != h[ss]:
                     p=rr; q=ss; ok=True; break
             if not ok: continue
          oldp=h[p]; oldq=h[q]
          if oldp == oldq: continue
          h[p]=oldq; h[q]=oldp
          c_new=counts_int(h)
          new=int(np.max(c_new))
          evals += 1
          if new < cur or (new == cur and rng.random() < 0.05):
             c=c_new; cur=new
             if new < best:
                best=new; best_h=h.copy(); last_best=evals
                if best == lb:
                   return best_h.astype(float), best*(2.0/n)
          else:
             h[p]=oldp; h[q]=oldq

       if evals - last_best > 5000:
          kick=best_h.copy()
          for __ in range(10):
             rr=int(rng.randint(n)); ss=int(rng.randint(n))
             if rr != ss and kick[rr] != kick[ss]:
                kick[rr],kick[ss]=kick[ss],kick[rr]
          h=kick
          c=counts_int(h)
          cur=int(np.max(c))
          top_infos=None
          last_best=evals
    return best_h.astype(float), best*(2.0/n)

def pair_local_search(h_in,n,rng,max_time=150.0):
    h=project_feasible(np.asarray(h_in,dtype=float),0.5*n)
    c=correlate_counts(h)
    cur=float(np.max(c))
    best=cur
    best_h=h.copy()
    start=time.time()
    evals=0; last_best=0; top_infos=None
    while time.time()-start < max_time and remaining_s()>20.0:
       if top_infos is None or evals % 400 == 0:
          off=n-1
          top_idx=np.argsort(c)[-6:]
          infos=[]
          for li in top_idx:
             lag=int(li)-off
             if lag==0: continue
             if lag>0:
                n0=np.arange(n-lag); a=n0+lag; b=n0
             else:
                d=-lag; n0=np.arange(d,n); a=n0+lag; b=n0
             infos.append((a,b))
          top_infos=infos
       for _ in range(60):
          if time.time()-start > max_time or remaining_s()<20.0: break
          p=-1; q=-1
          if top_infos is not None and len(top_infos)>0 and rng.random()<0.75:
             a,b=top_infos[int(rng.randint(len(top_infos)))]
             sample_count=min(6,max(1,a.size))
             idxs=np.unique(rng.randint(0,a.size, size=sample_count))
             best_s=-1.0
             for idx in idxs:
                idx=int(idx)
                s=h[a[idx]]*(1.0-h[b[idx]])
                if s>best_s:
                   best_s=s; p=int(a[idx]); q=int(b[idx])
             if best_s <= 1e-10 or h[p] <= h[q]:
                p=-1; q=-1
          if p < 0:
             ok=False
             for __ in range(10):
                rr=int(rng.randint(n)); ss=int(rng.randint(n))
                if rr != ss and h[rr] > h[ss] + 1e-8:
                   p=rr; q=ss; ok=True; break
             if not ok: continue
          max_alpha=min(float(h[p]), float(1.0-h[q]))
          if max_alpha <= 1e-8: continue
          oldp=float(h[p]); oldq=float(h[q])
          best_alpha=None; best_new=cur + 1e-9; best_c_new=None
          for frac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
             alpha=frac*max_alpha
             h[p]=oldp-alpha; h[q]=oldq+alpha
             c_new=correlate_counts(h)
             new=float(np.max(c_new))
             evals += 1
             if new < best_new:
                best_new=new; best_alpha=alpha; best_c_new=c_new
          h[p]=oldp; h[q]=oldq
          if best_alpha is not None and (best_new < cur - 1e-12 or (best_new == cur and rng.random() < 0.05)):
             h[p]=oldp-best_alpha; h[q]=oldq+best_alpha
             c=best_c_new; cur=best_new
             if best_new < best:
                best=best_new; best_h=h.copy(); last_best=evals
       if evals - last_best > 6000:
          kick_h=best_h.copy()
          for __ in range(8):
             rr=int(rng.randint(n)); ss=int(rng.randint(n))
             if rr != ss and kick_h[rr] > kick_h[ss] + 1e-8:
                # transfer a random fraction
                trans = min(0.25, 0.25*(kick_h[rr]-kick_h[ss]))
                if trans > 1e-8:
                   kick_h[rr] -= trans; kick_h[ss] += trans
          kick_h=project_feasible(kick_h,0.5*n)
          h=kick_h
          c=correlate_counts(h)
          cur=float(np.max(c))
          top_infos=None
          last_best=evals
    return best_h, best*(2.0/n)
```

def large_smooth_track(rng, max_time=500.0):
   parent=_STATE.get("parent_h")
   if parent is None: parent=_STATE.get("best_h")
   if parent is None: return
   parent=np.asarray(parent,dtype=float)
   n_parent=parent.size
   if n_parent %2 !=0: n_parent+=1
   if n_parent <128: n_main=160
   elif n_parent >384: n_main=320
   else: n_main=n_parent
   deadline=min(time.time()+max_time, time.time()+remaining_s()-60.0)
   if deadline <= time.time()+60: return
   h_base=interp_h(parent,n_main)
   starts=[h_base, symmetrize(h_base,n_main), binary_from_float(h_base,n_main).astype(float), make_lowfreq_continuous(n_main,rng,4)]
   betas=[20.0,60.0,160.0,400.0]
   iters=[200,250,300,350]
   for h0 in starts:
      if time.time() >= deadline or remaining_s()<100.0: break

      try: run_smooth_sequence(h_base,n_main,[60.0,160.0],[220,260],False,deadline=deadline)
      except Exception: pass
   if time.time() < deadline - 50.0 and remaining_s()>80.0:
      h0=symmetrize(h_base,n_main)

def binary_local_track(rng, max_time=400.0):
    start=time.time(); deadline=start+max_time
    ns=[12,14,16,18,20,24,28,32,40,48,64,96,128,160,200,256]
    # insert current best n if even
    bn=_STATE.get("best_n",0)
    if bn %2==0 and bn not in ns and 12 <= bn <= 320:
        ns.insert(0,bn)
    for n in ns:
        if time.time() >= deadline or remaining_s()<80.0: break
        per_n = max(15.0, min(60.0, deadline-time.time()))
        # if best already very low, skip large n?
        if _STATE["best_c"] < 0.30 and n > 128: continue
        starts=[]

            starts.append(binary_from_float(interp_h(_STATE["best_h"], n), n))
        starts.append(make_lowfreq_binary(n, rng, 5))
        starts.append(make_lowfreq_binary(n, rng, 6))

            try:

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
    _STATE["start"]=time.time()
    _STATE["budget"]=float(budget_s)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    _STATE["best_h"]=None; _STATE["best_c"]=float("inf"); _STATE["best_n"]=0; _STATE["parent_h"]=None
    try:
        parent=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float)
        if parent.size>0:
            parent=project_feasible(parent,0.5*parent.size)
            _STATE["parent_h"]=parent.copy()
            update_best(parent,parent.size)
            hs=symmetrize(parent,parent.size); update_best(hs,parent.size)
            hb=binary_from_float(parent,parent.size); update_best(hb,parent.size)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if _STATE["best_h"] is None:
        n0=200
        h0=make_lowfreq_continuous(n0,rng,5)
        update_best(h0,n0)
        _STATE["parent_h"]=h0.copy()

    # small n
    try:
       small_n_track(rng)
    except Exception:
       pass

    # large smooth track
    if remaining_s() > 150.0:
       allow = 550.0 if _STATE["best_c"] > _TARGET else 220.0
       allow = min(allow, max(60.0, remaining_s()-120.0))
       if allow > 80.0:
          try: large_smooth_track(rng, allow)
          except Exception: pass

    # binary local track
    if remaining_s() > 120.0:
       allow = min(400.0, max(60.0, remaining_s()-120.0))
       if _STATE["best_c"] < 0.30:
           allow = min(allow, 120.0)
       if allow > 30.0:
           try: binary_local_track(rng, allow)
           except Exception: pass

    # continuous pair local search
    if remaining_s() > 60.0:
       best_h=_STATE["best_h"]; n_best=_STATE["best_n"]
       maxt=min(350.0, remaining_s()*0.55)
       if maxt > 40.0:
          try:
             h,c=pair_local_search(best_h,n_best,rng,maxt)
             update_best(h,n_best,c)
          except Exception:
             pass
       if remaining_s() > 60.0:
          best_h=_STATE["best_h"]; n_best=_STATE["best_n"]
          hs=symmetrize(best_h,n_best)
          maxt=min(140.0, remaining_s()*0.55)
          if maxt > 20.0:
             try:
                h,c=pair_local_search(hs,n_best,rng,maxt)
                update_best(h,n_best,c)
             except Exception:
                pass

    # final small n local maybe if remaining and current not very low
    if remaining_s() > 90.0 and _STATE["best_c"] > _TARGET:
       try:
          allow=min(120.0, remaining_s()-30.0)
          if allow > 30.0:
             binary_local_track(rng, allow)
             small_n_track(rng)
       except Exception:
          pass

    best_h=np.asarray(_STATE["best_h"],dtype=float)
    best_n=int(_STATE["best_n"])
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,0.5*best_n)
    final_c=compute_c5(best_h)
    try:
        eval_c=evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        eval_c=float(eval_c)
        if eval_c < final_c:
            final_c=eval_c
    except Exception:
        pass
    print("FINAL: n={}, C5={:.6f}".format(best_n, final_c))
    return (best_h, float(final_c), best_n)

If h is sym but due project full, first half sum maybe n/4? It should because sym h and sum n/2 => pair sums total n/4 if n even? If h symmetric exactly, yes. If not, projection y fixes.

Potential bug: SLSQP may have trouble with integer target for sym if n not multiple of4 and y all 0/1? But continuous y feasible.

Potential bug: `smooth_sym_obj` uses y that may violate constraints during minimize? SLSQP handles.

Potential bug: `full_sum_jac` shape (1,n). SLSQP expects (m,n) or (n,) maybe okay. If it requires (n,), (1,n) might error? I think SLSQP accepts matrix. To be safe, maybe return 1D array. The previous code used con_fun scalar and no jac. Let's use 1D jac to be safe? Documentation: Jacobian of constraint should be shape (m,n) or (n,) for scalar. For scalar constraint, (n,) likely accepted. Let's return `np.ones_like(x)` and fun scalar/array? If fun returns array length1, jac shape (1,n) consistent. I think SciPy SLSQP handles 2D. Okay.

Potential bug: If parent h length odd and binary_from_float gives sum floor(n/2), not feasible. update_best projects to sum n/2, converting fractional? For odd n, binary project may make fractional one value to meet n/2 (e.g., half). Fine. But we mainly use even n.

Potential bug: `binary_local_track` calls `interp_h(_STATE["best_h"], n)` where best_h may be length n already; okay. It then binary_from_float to exactly n/2 ones (floor if odd). n even.

Potential bug: `binary_from_float` for h length n and n even. Good.

Potential bug: small_nTrack continuous calls `run_smooth_sequence(h0,n,betas,iters,False,deadline=deadline)` but `deadline` passed computed at function start; if one SLSQP takes long, next may not start. Fine.

Potential bug: global `_STATE` best updated in `update_best` recomputes exact c, which can be slightly different than passed. If passed binary c lower but compute returns same. Good.

Now, do we need to output exactly one fenced code block. Yes.

Let's produce final corrected code. Ensure all functions top-level. No nested functions. Use no lambdas.

One more optimization: In `grad_from_weights`, for each d, if both wp and wn zero skip; but doing `if wn != 0.0` for float weights could be many tiny. Use threshold:
```
if wn > 1e-14: ...
if wp > 1e-14: ...
```
Good.

In `smooth_max_value_and_weights`, if beta is 0? no.

Let's final.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize
from itertools import combinations

_STATE = {
    "start": 0.0,
    "budget": 1300.0,
    "best_h": None,
    "best_c": float("inf"),
    "best_n": 0,
    "parent_h": None,
}
_SYM_N = 200
_TARGET = 0.38085


def remaining_s():
    return max(0.0, _STATE["budget"] - (time.time() - _STATE["start"]))


def update_best(h, n, c=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return
    h = project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n)
    if c is None:
        c = compute_c5(h)
    else:
        c = min(float(c), compute_c5(h))
    if c + 1e-15 < _STATE["best_c"]:
        _STATE["best_h"] = h.copy()
        _STATE["best_c"] = float(c)
        _STATE["best_n"] = n
        print("improved: n={}, c5={:.6f}".format(n, c))


def project_feasible(h, target=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return h
    if target is None:
        target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    s_lo = np.clip(h + lo, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
    s_hi = np.clip(h + hi, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
    while s_lo > target and lo > -10.0:
        lo *= 2.0
        s_lo = np.clip(h + lo, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
    while s_hi < target and hi < 10.0:
        hi *= 2.0
        s_hi = np.clip(h + hi, 0.0, 1.0).sum()

    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid

    h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        slack = np.minimum(h, 1.0 - h)
        idx = int(np.argmax(slack))
        if slack[idx] > abs(diff) * 1.001:
            h[idx] += diff
        else:
            mask = slack > 1e-12
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt > 0:
                h[mask] += diff / cnt
            else:
                h[0] += diff
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        slack = np.minimum(h, 1.0 - h)
        idx = int(np.argmax(slack))
        h[idx] = float(np.clip(h[idx] + diff, 0.0, 1.0))
    return h


def symmetrize(h, n=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size) if n is None else int(n)
    s = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    return project_feasible(s, 0.5 * n)


def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr) * (2.0 / n))


def correlate_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def counts_int(h):
    h16 = h.astype(np.int16, copy=False)
    v16 = (1 - h16).astype(np.int16, copy=False)
    return np.correlate(h16, v16, mode="full")


def interp_h(h_src, n_dst):
    h_src = np.asarray(h_src, dtype=float)
    n_src = h_src.size
    n_dst = int(n_dst)
    if n_src == 0:
        return np.zeros(n_dst, dtype=float)
    if n_src == n_dst:
        return project_feasible(h_src.copy(), 0.5 * n_dst)
    xs = (np.arange(n_src) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_src)
    xd = (np.arange(n_dst) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_dst)
    h = np.interp(xd, xs, h_src)
    return project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n_dst)


def binary_from_float(h, n=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size) if n is None else int(n)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return out
    if m >= n:
        out.fill(1.0)
        return out
    idx = np.argpartition(h, -m)[-m:]
    out[idx] = 1.0
    return out


def binary_from_mask(mask, n):
    h = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=float)
    for i in range(int(n)):
        if (mask >> i) & 1:
            h[i] = 1.0
    return h


def make_lowfreq_continuous(n, rng, modes=4):
    t = (np.arange(int(n)) + 0.5) / float(n)
    h = np.full(int(n), 0.5, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(int(modes)):
        f = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 4.0))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.12, 0.55))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        sgn = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
        h += sgn * amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + ph)
    return project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n)


def make_lowfreq_binary(n, rng, modes=5):
    h = make_lowfreq_continuous(int(n), rng, modes)
    return binary_from_float(h, int(n))


def smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, beta):
    corr = np.asarray(corr, dtype=float)
    beta = float(beta)
    m = float(np.max(corr))
    scaled = beta * (corr - m)
    np.clip(scaled, -50.0, 0.0, out=scaled)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    total = float(exps.sum())
    val = m + np.log(total) / beta
    w = exps / total
    return val, w


def grad_from_weights(h, weights):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    off = n - 1
    w0 = weights[off]
    if abs(w0) > 1e-15:
        g += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    one_h = 1.0 - h
    for d in range(1, n):
        wp = weights[off + d]
        wn = weights[off - d]
        if wp <= 1e-14 and wn <= 1e-14:
            continue
        a = n - d
        if wn > 1e-14:
            g[:a] += wn * one_h[d:]
        if wp > 1e-14:
            g[:a] -= wp * h[d:]
        if wp > 1e-14:
            g[d:] += wp * one_h[:a]
        if wn > 1e-14:
            g[d:] -= wn * h[:a]
    return g


def full_obj(x, beta):
    n = x.size
    corr = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    val, _ = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, float(beta))
    return val * (2.0 / n)


def full_jac(x, beta):
    n = x.size
    corr = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    _, w = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, float(beta))
    g = grad_from_weights(x, w)
    return g * (2.0 / n)


def full_sum_fun(x):
    return np.array([np.sum(x) - 0.5 * x.size])


def full_sum_jac(x):
    return np.ones((1, x.size))


def make_h_sym(y, n):
    m = n // 2
    h = np.empty(int(n), dtype=float)
    h[:m] = y
    h[m:] = y[::-1]
    return h


def smooth_sym_obj(y, beta, n):
    h = make_h_sym(y, int(n))
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    val, _ = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, float(beta))
    return val * (2.0 / n)


def smooth_sym_jac(y, beta, n):
    n = int(n)
    h = make_h_sym(y, n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    _, w = smooth_max_value_and_weights(corr, float(beta))
    gf = grad_from_weights(h, w)
    m = n // 2
    g = gf[:m] + gf[m:][::-1]
    return g * (2.0 / n)


def sym_sum_fun(y):
    return np.array([np.sum(y) - 0.25 * _SYM_N])


def sym_sum_jac(y):
    return np.ones((1, y.size))


def run_slsqp_single(h0, n, beta, maxiter, sym):
    h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    beta = float(beta)
    it = max(1, int(maxiter))
    sym = bool(sym) and (n % 2 == 0) and n >= 4
    res = None

    if sym:
        m = n // 2
        target = 0.25 * n
        y = h0[:m].copy()
        if y.size != m:
            y = np.resize(y, m)
        y = project_feasible(y, target)
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m
        cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sym_sum_fun, "jac": sym_sum_jac}]
        _SYM_N = n
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_sym_obj,
                y,
                args=(beta, n),
                jac=smooth_sym_jac,
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": it, "ftol": 1e-12},
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

        yf = getattr(res, "x", None) if res is not None else None
        if yf is None:
            yf = y
        y = project_feasible(np.asarray(yf, dtype=float), target)
        h = make_h_sym(y, n)
        h = project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n)
        h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
        h = project_feasible(h, 0.5 * n)
    else:
        h = project_feasible(h0, 0.5 * n)
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
        cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": full_sum_fun, "jac": full_sum_jac}]
        try:
            res = minimize(
                full_obj,
                h,
                args=(beta,),
                jac=full_jac,
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": it, "ftol": 1e-12},
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

        xf = getattr(res, "x", None) if res is not None else None
        if xf is None:
            xf = h
        h = project_feasible(np.asarray(xf, dtype=float), 0.5 * n)

    return h, compute_c5(h)


def run_smooth_sequence(h0, n, betas, iters, sym, deadline=0.0):
    h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return h0, float("inf")
    h = project_feasible(h0, 0.5 * n)
    c = compute_c5(h)
    update_best(h, n, c)

    for i in range(len(betas)):
        if deadline > 0.0 and time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if remaining_s() < 45.0:
            break
        try:
            nh, nc = run_slsqp_single(h, n, betas[i], iters[i], sym)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if nc < c - 1e-13:
            h = nh
            c = nc
            update_best(h, n, c)
    return h, c


def enumerate_binary_counts(n, max_time=20.0):
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return 0, None
    limit_mask = (1 << n) - 1
    best = n + 1
    best_mask = None
    lb = int(np.ceil((n * n) / (4.0 * (2.0 * n - 2.0)))) if n > 1 else 0
    start = time.time()

    for comb in combinations(range(n), m):
        if (time.time() - start) > max_time:
            break
        if remaining_s() < 20.0:
            break
        mask = 0
        for ii in comb:
            mask |= 1 << ii
        z = limit_mask ^ mask
        maxc = 0
        for d in range(1, n):
            pc = (mask & (z >> d)).bit_count()
            if pc > maxc:
                maxc = pc
            if maxc >= best:
                break
            qc = (mask & ((z << d) & limit_mask)).bit_count()
            if qc > maxc:
                maxc = qc
            if maxc >= best:
                break
        if maxc < best:
            best = maxc
            best_mask = mask
            if best == lb:
                break
    return best, best_mask


def small_n_track(rng, max_time=None):
    if max_time is None:
        max_time = min(200.0, 0.18 * _STATE["budget"])
    max_time = max(10.0, float(max_time))
    deadline = time.time() + max_time
    ns = [6, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20]

    for n in ns:
        if time.time() > deadline or remaining_s() < 80.0:
            break
        cnt = None
        mask = None
        if n <= 20:
            et = 10.0 if n <= 14 else 25.0
            et = max(4.0, min(et, deadline - time.time() - 60.0))
            if et > 3.0 and remaining_s() > et + 40.0:
                try:
                    cnt, mask = enumerate_binary_counts(n, et)
                    if mask is not None and cnt < n:
                        h = binary_from_mask(mask, n)
                        update_best(h, n, cnt * 2.0 / n)
                except Exception:
                    cnt, mask = None, None

        if time.time() > deadline - 80.0 or remaining_s() < 70.0:
            continue

        starts = []
        if mask is not None:
            starts.append(binary_from_mask(mask, n))
        starts.append(make_lowfreq_continuous(n, rng, 5))
        if _STATE["best_h"] is not None and _STATE["best_h"].size > 1:
            starts.append(interp_h(_STATE["best_h"], n))

        betas = [20.0, 60.0, 150.0]
        iters = [120, 150, 180]
        for h0 in starts[:3]:
            if time.time() > deadline - 30.0 or remaining_s() < 50.0:
                break
            try:
                run_smooth_sequence(h0, n, betas, iters, False, deadline=deadline)
            except Exception:
                pass


def local_binary_search(h_in, n, rng, max_time=60.0):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.asarray(h_in, dtype=np.int8).copy()
    m = n // 2
    if int(np.sum(h)) != m:
        h = binary_from_float(h.astype(float), n).astype(np.int8)

    c = counts_int(h)
    cur = int(np.max(c))
    best = cur
    best_h = h.copy()
    start = time.time()
    evals = 0
    last_best = 0
    top_infos = None
    lb = int(np.ceil((n * n) / (4.0 * (2.0 * n - 2.0)))) if n > 1 else 0

    while time.time() - start < max_time and remaining_s() > 20.0:
        if top_infos is None or evals % 300 == 0:
            off = n - 1
            top_idx = np.argsort(c)[-6:]
            infos = []
            for li in top_idx:
                li = int(li)
                lag = li - off
                if lag == 0:
                    continue
                if lag > 0:
                    n0 = np.arange(n - lag)
                    a = n0 + lag
                    b = n0
                else:
                    d = -lag
                    n0 = np.arange(d, n)
                    a = n0 + lag
                    b = n0
                infos.append((a, b))
            top_infos = infos

        for _ in range(250):
            if time.time() - start > max_time or remaining_s() < 20.0:
                break
            p = -1
            q = -1

            if top_infos is not None and len(top_infos) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.75:
                a, b = top_infos[int(rng.randint(len(top_infos)))]
                bad = (h[a] == 1) & (h[b] == 0)
                if np.any(bad):
                    pos = np.where(bad)[0]
                    sel = pos[int(rng.randint(len(pos)))]
                    p = int(a[sel])
                    q = int(b[sel])

            if p < 0:
                ok = False
                for __ in range(10):
                    rr = int(rng.randint(n))
                    ss = int(rng.randint(n))
                    if rr != ss and h[rr] != h[ss]:
                        p = rr
                        q = ss
                        ok = True
                        break
                if not ok:
                    continue

            oldp = h[p]
            oldq = h[q]
            if oldp == oldq:
                continue

            h[p] = oldq
            h[q] = oldp
            c_new = counts_int(h)
            new = int(np.max(c_new))
            evals += 1

            if new < cur or (new == cur and rng.random() < 0.05):
                c = c_new
                cur = new
                if new < best:
                    best = new
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    last_best = evals
                    if best == lb:
                        return best_h.astype(float), best * (2.0 / n)
            else:
                h[p] = oldp
                h[q] = oldq

        if evals - last_best > 5000:
            kick = best_h.copy()
            for __ in range(10):
                rr = int(rng.randint(n))
                ss = int(rng.randint(n))
                if rr != ss and kick[rr] != kick[ss]:
                    kick[rr], kick[ss] = kick[ss], kick[rr]
            h = kick
            c = counts_int(h)
            cur = int(np.max(c))
            top_infos = None
            last_best = evals

    return best_h.astype(float), best * (2.0 / n)


def pair_local_search(h_in, n, rng, max_time=150.0):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h_in, dtype=float), 0.5 * n)
    c = correlate_counts(h)
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    best = cur
    best_h = h.copy()
    start = time.time()
    evals = 0
    last_best = 0
    top_infos = None

    while time.time() - start < max_time and remaining_s() > 20.0:
        if top_infos is None or evals % 400 == 0:
            off = n - 1
            top_idx = np.argsort(c)[-6:]
            infos = []
            for li in top_idx:
                li = int(li)
                lag = li - off
                if lag == 0:
                    continue
                if lag > 0:
                    n0 = np.arange(n - lag)
                    a = n0 + lag
                    b = n0
                else:
                    d = -lag
                    n0 = np.arange(d, n)
                    a = n0 + lag
                    b = n0
                infos.append((a, b))
            top_infos = infos

        for _ in range(60):
            if time.time() - start > max_time or remaining_s() < 20.0:
                break
            p = -1
            q = -1

            if top_infos is not None and len(top_infos) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.75:
                a, b = top_infos[int(rng.randint(len(top_infos)))]
                sample_count = min(6, max(1, int(a.size)))
                idxs = np.unique(rng.randint(0, int(a.size), size=sample_count))
                best_s = -1.0
                for idx in idxs:
                    idx = int(idx)
                    s = float(h[a[idx]] * (1.0 - h[b[idx]]))
                    if s > best_s:
                        best_s = s
                        p = int(a[idx])
                        q = int(b[idx])
                if best_s <= 1e-10 or h[p] <= h[q]:
                    p = -1
                    q = -1

            if p < 0:
                ok = False
                for __ in range(10):
                    rr = int(rng.randint(n))
                    ss = int(rng.randint(n))
                    if rr != ss and h[rr] > h[ss] + 1e-8:
                        p = rr
                        q = ss
                        ok = True
                        break
                if not ok:
                    continue

            max_alpha = min(float(h[p]), float(1.0 - h[q]))
            if max_alpha <= 1e-8:
                continue

            oldp = float(h[p])
            oldq = float(h[q])
            best_alpha = None
            best_new = cur + 1e-9
            best_c_new = None

            for frac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
                alpha = frac * max_alpha
                h[p] = oldp - alpha
                h[q] = oldq + alpha
                c_new = correlate_counts(h)
                new = float(np.max(c_new))
                evals += 1
                if new < best_new:
                    best_new = new
                    best_alpha = alpha
                    best_c_new = c_new

            h[p] = oldp
            h[q] = oldq

            if best_alpha is not None and (best_new < cur - 1e-12 or (best_new == cur and rng.random() < 0.05)):
                h[p] = oldp - best_alpha
                h[q] = oldq + best_alpha
                c = best_c_new
                cur = best_new
                if best_new < best:
                    best = best_new
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    last_best = evals

        if evals - last_best > 6000:
            kick_h = best_h.copy()
            for __ in range(8):
                rr = int(rng.randint(n))
                ss = int(rng.randint(n))
                if rr != ss and kick_h[rr] > kick_h[ss] + 1e-8:
                    trans = min(0.25, 0.25 * (float(kick_h[rr]) - float(kick_h[ss])))
                    if trans > 1e-8:
                        kick_h[rr] -= trans
                        kick_h[ss] += trans
            kick_h = project_feasible(kick_h, 0.5 * n)
            h = kick_h
            c = correlate_counts(h)
            cur = float(np.max(c))
            top_infos = None
            last_best = evals

    return best_h, best * (2.0 / n)


def large_smooth_track(rng, max_time=500.0):
    parent = _STATE.get("parent_h", None)
    if parent is None:
        parent = _STATE.get("best_h", None)
    if parent is None:
        return
    parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
    n_parent = parent.size
    if n_parent % 2 != 0:
        n_parent += 1
    if n_parent < 128:
        n_main = 160
    elif n_parent > 384:
        n_main = 320
    else:
        n_main = n_parent

    deadline = min(time.time() + float(max_time), time.time() + remaining_s() - 60.0)
    if deadline <= time.time() + 60.0:
        return

    h_base = interp_h(parent, n_main)
    starts = [
        h_base,
        symmetrize(h_base, n_main),
        binary_from_float(h_base, n_main).astype(float),
        make_lowfreq_continuous(n_main, rng, 4),
    ]
    betas = [20.0, 60.0, 160.0, 400.0]
    iters = [200, 250, 300, 350]

    for h0 in starts:
        if time.time() >= deadline or remaining_s() < 100.0:
            break
        try:
            run_smooth_sequence(h0, n_main, betas, iters, True, deadline=deadline)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 100.0 and remaining_s() > 120.0:
        try:
            run_smooth_sequence(h_base, n_main, [60.0, 160.0], [220, 260], False, deadline=deadline)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 50.0 and remaining_s() > 80.0:
        h0 = symmetrize(h_base, n_main)
        try:
            run_smooth_sequence(h0, n_main, [100.0, 250.0], [220, 220], False, deadline=deadline)
        except Exception:
            pass


def binary_local_track(rng, max_time=400.0):
    start = time.time()
    deadline = start + float(max_time)
    ns = [12, 14, 16, 18, 20, 24, 28, 32, 40, 48, 64, 96, 128, 160, 200, 256]

    bn = int(_STATE.get("best_n", 0))
    if bn % 2 == 0 and 12 <= bn <= 320 and bn not in ns:
        ns.insert(0, bn)

    for n in ns:
        if time.time() >= deadline or remaining_s() < 80.0:
            break
        if _STATE["best_c"] < 0.30 and n > 128:
            continue

        starts = []
        if _STATE.get("best_h") is not None:
            starts.append(binary_from_float(interp_h(_STATE["best_h"], n), n))
        starts.append(make_lowfreq_binary(n, rng, 5))
        starts.append(make_lowfreq_binary(n, rng, 6))
        if _STATE.get("parent_h") is not None:
            starts.append(binary_from_float(interp_h(_STATE["parent_h"], n), n))

        for h0 in starts[:3]:
            if time.time() >= deadline or remaining_s() < 50.0:
                break
            tt = min(30.0, max(10.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.25))
            if tt <= 1.0:
                break
            try:
                h, c = local_binary_search(h0, n, rng, tt)
                update_best(h, n, c)
            except Exception:
                pass


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    _STATE["start"] = time.time()
    _STATE["budget"] = float(budget_s)
    _STATE["best_h"] = None
    _STATE["best_c"] = float("inf")
    _STATE["best_n"] = 0
    _STATE["parent_h"] = None
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if parent.size > 0:
            parent = project_feasible(parent, 0.5 * parent.size)
            _STATE["parent_h"] = parent.copy()
            update_best(parent, parent.size)
            hs = symmetrize(parent, parent.size)
            update_best(hs, parent.size)
            hb = binary_from_float(parent, parent.size)
            update_best(hb, parent.size)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if _STATE["best_h"] is None:
        n0 = 200
        h0 = make_lowfreq_continuous(n0, rng, 5)
        update_best(h0, n0)
        _STATE["parent_h"] = h0.copy()

    try:
        small_n_track(rng, 200.0)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if remaining_s() > 150.0:
        allow = 550.0 if _STATE["best_c"] > _TARGET else 220.0
        allow = min(allow, max(60.0, remaining_s() - 120.0))
        if allow > 80.0:
            try:
                large_smooth_track(rng, allow)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if remaining_s() > 120.0:
        allow = min(400.0, max(60.0, remaining_s() - 120.0))
        if _STATE["best_c"] < 0.30:
            allow = min(allow, 120.0)
        if allow > 30.0:
            try:
                binary_local_track(rng, allow)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if remaining_s() > 60.0:
        best_h = _STATE["best_h"]
        n_best = int(_STATE["best_n"])
        maxt = min(350.0, remaining_s() * 0.55)
        if maxt > 40.0:
            try:
                h, c = pair_local_search(best_h, n_best, rng, maxt)
                update_best(h, n_best, c)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if remaining_s() > 60.0:
        best_h = _STATE["best_h"]
        n_best = int(_STATE["best_n"])
        hs = symmetrize(best_h, n_best)
        maxt = min(140.0, remaining_s() * 0.55)
        if maxt > 20.0:
            try:
                h, c = pair_local_search(hs, n_best, rng, maxt)
                update_best(h, n_best, c)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if remaining_s() > 90.0 and _STATE["best_c"] > _TARGET:
        allow = min(120.0, remaining_s() - 30.0)
        if allow > 30.0:
            try:
                binary_local_track(rng, max(20.0, allow * 0.5))
                small_n_track(rng, max(20.0, allow * 0.5))
            except Exception:
                pass

    best_h = np.asarray(_STATE["best_h"], dtype=float)
    best_n = int(_STATE["best_n"])
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, 0.5 * best_n)
    final_c = compute_c5(best_h)

    try:
        eval_c = float(evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h))
        if eval_c < final_c:
            final_c = eval_c
    except Exception:
        pass

    print("FINAL: n={}, C5={:.6f}".format(best_n, final_c))
    return (best_h, float(final_c), best_n)
```
